## Chapter 5

###### Tractate Soferim 5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/5:1)


הכותב שם של שני שמות של קודש מקיים את הראשון ומעכב את האחרון ר׳ יהודה אומר אם הראשון בסוף הדף מעכב את הראשון ומקיים את האחרון שני שמות של חול מקיים את הראשון ומוחק את האחרון ר' יהודה אומר אם היה הראשון בראש הדף מוחק את הראשון ומקיים את האחרון:


If one wrote a sacred name twice,¹ he retains the first and erases the latter.² R. Judah says: If the first occurs at the end of a column, he erases the first and retains the second.³ [If he wrote] a secular noun twice,⁴ he retains the first and wipes away the latter.⁵ R. Judah says: If the first was at the end⁶ of a column, he wipes away the first and retains the latter.

footnotes:
¹ lit. ‘he who writes a name of two sacred names’. H omits ‘a name of’.
² The first is retained because it was properly written; the second is erased because it was written in error.
³ It spoils the appearance to leave a blank at the beginning of the top line of a column.
⁴ lit. ‘two secular nouns’.
⁵ While the ink is still wet.
⁶ So GRA. V and H, ‘at the top’.


###### Tractate Soferim 5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/5:2)


הכותב יהודה ולא נתן בו דלת יתלנו מלמעלה. את השם כותב יהודה עושה מדלת הי ומוחק הי האחרון היה צריך לכתוב יהודה ונתכוון וכתב את השם. אע"פ שלא כתב בו דלת מעכבו וכותב יהודה אחר:


If one writes *Yehudah* and omits the *daleth*, he inserts⁷ and the *he*. it above the line. If he intended to write the Tetragrammaton⁸, lit. ‘the name’. and wrote *Yehudah*⁹ instead,¹⁰ he alters the *daleth* into a *he*¹¹. and wipes away the final letter *he*. If it was required to write *Yehudah* and he intended to write the Tetragrammaton, although he [inadvertently] inserted the *daleth*,¹² he erases it¹³ and writes another *Yehudah*.

footnotes:
⁷ lit. ‘suspends’ between the waw
⁸ The four letters Yod-he-waw-he
⁹ יהודה.
¹⁰ This is the reading of GRA and H. V omits ‘if he intended to write’,
¹¹ This is done by adding a short vertical line to the daleth
¹² So H. V reads: ‘he did not write in it’.
¹³ Following GRA. V and H ‘detains it’.


###### Tractate Soferim 5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/5:3)


היה צריך לכתוב את השם ונתכוון וכתב יהודה ולא נתן בו דלת מוחקו וכותב את השם ר׳ יהודה אומר יחזור עליו את הקולמוס ויקדשנו אמרו לו אין זה מן המובחר:


If it was required to write the Tetragrammaton but he intended to write *Yehudah*, although he did not insert the *daleth*¹⁴ he must wipe away [the entire word] and re-write the Tetragrammaton. R. Judah says: He should go over it with the reed-pen and so sanctify it. [The Sages] said to him: This is not the best procedure.

footnotes:
¹⁴ So that the Tetragrammaton was written correctly but unintentionally so.


###### Tractate Soferim 5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/5:4)


הכותב את השם וטעה בה אות אחת יתלנו מלמעלה רבי שמעון שזורי אומר שם כולו תולין אות אחת ממנו אין תולין טעה את כל השם יתלנו בין השיטין דברי ר׳ יהודה רבי מאיר אומר מוחק מלפניו וכותב את השם על מה שמחק רבי שמעון בן אלעזר אומר משמו אין תולין אלא השם ואין כותבין האזכרות על מקום הגרר ר׳ חנינא בן עקביא אומר יגנז את כל היריעה:


He who writes the divine name and made an error [by omitting] one letter should suspend it above [the line].¹⁵ R. Simeon of Shezur says: All the name may be suspended [if omitted] but a single letter of it may not be suspended. If one erred [by omitting] all the name, he should suspend it between the lines, according to the view of R. Judah. R. Meir says: He wipes away the writing that precedes and writes the name on the part which he wiped away. R. Simeon b. Eleazar said in the name of R. Meir: The name only may be suspended but the Tetragrammaton must not be written on an erasure. R. Ḥanina b. ‘Aḳabia says: The whole sheet¹⁶ must be stored away.¹⁷

footnotes:
¹⁵ Neither erasure nor wiping away of the written words is necessary.
¹⁶ In which the omission of the Tetragrammaton had occurred.
¹⁷ As with all sacred objects that are worn out or decaying, or are for any other reason unfit for further use. GRA revised the wording of this Rule to conform in the main with the text in Men. 30b (Sonc. ed., p. 196): ‘If the Tetragrammaton was omitted in error [and a number of the words following it already written], what had been written is erased, the erased words are suspended [above the line] and the Tetragrammaton is inserted in the space of the erasure; so R. Judah. R. Meir said: He wipes the writing away and writes [the Tetragrammaton] in the space. R. Jose said: Even the Tetragrammaton may be suspended [above the line]. R. Simeon of Shezur said: It is permissible to suspend all the Tetragrammaton [above the line] but not a part of it. R. Simeon b. Eleazar said in the name of R. Meir: The Tetragrammaton may not be written in a space which has been erased or wiped away, nor may it be suspended [above the line]; but the entire sheet is removed and stored away’.


###### Tractate Soferim 5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/5:5)


טעה וכתב יסגירני אל אל עויל (איוב ט״ז:י״א) מקיים את הראשון ומעכב את האחרון:


If one wrote three¹⁸ times *Alef-lamed* by mistake in the phrase *God delivereth me to the ungodly*,¹⁹ he retains the first and erases the second.²⁰ were meant to be the divine name and Rule 1 above applies. By quoting an illustration from Job, the inference to be drawn is that the regulation with respect to a Torah scroll holds good also of the Prophets and Hagiographa.

footnotes:
¹⁸ So GRA. V ‘twice’.
¹⁹ Job 16, 11. The text has the word twice, the first being the divine name and the second the preposition; inadvertently he wrote it three times, intending the first two to stand for ‘God’ (thus sanctifying both) and the third for ‘to’.
²⁰ So GRA and H. The first occurrences of ’el


###### Tractate Soferim 5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/5:6)


הכותב את השם אפי׳ המלך שואל בשלומו לא ישיבנו. היה כותב שנים ושלשה שמות כאחת מפסיק ביניהם ומשיב הטובל קולמוס לא יתחיל אותיות השם אלא מתחיל מאות שלפניו המוחק אות אחת מן השם עובר בלא תעשה א"ר שמעון לכך נאמר (דברים יב) ואבדתם את שמם מן המקום ההוא לא תעשון כן לה׳ אלהיכם:


When one is writing the Tetragrammaton he may not respond even if the king greets him.²¹ If he was about to write two or three divine names successively,²² in Ex. 34, 6. he may pause between them and respond. After dipping the reed-pen in ink, he may not start with the letters of a divine name²³ but with the preceding letter.²⁴ One who erases a single letter of a divine name transgresses a negative commandment. R. Simeon said:²⁵ On account of this it is stated, *Ye shall destroy their name out of that place. Ye shall not do so unto the Lord your God*.²⁶

footnotes:
²¹ All his attention must be concentrated on the sanctity of the divine name.
²² E.g. The Lord, the Lord, God
²³ Because a pen full of ink may cause a blot.
²⁴ Which, if blotted, can be erased or wiped away.
²⁵ Citing the Scriptural basis of the regulation.
²⁶ Deut. 12, 3f, i.e. the name of God must not be erased.


###### Tractate Soferim 5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/5:7)


אבל הדיו שנטפה על גבי הכתב מותר למוחקו שלא היתה כוונתו אלא לתקן:


It is permissible, however, to wipe away ink that dropped upon the writing,²⁷ since one’s intention is only to rectify it.

footnotes:
²⁷ Even of a divine name.


###### Tractate Soferim 5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/5:8)


הטועה את השיטה אינו תולה (אלא) מנגד השיטין אלא הטעות גורר אחת וכותב שתים או שתים וכותב שלש ובלבד שלא יגרד שלש :


If one omits a line in error, he may not suspend it between the lines, but inserts it in close proximity²⁸ to the place²⁹ of the error by erasing one [adjacent line] and inserting two³⁰ in its place, or [by erasing] two lines and writing three;³¹ provided only that he does not erase three lines.³²

footnotes:
²⁸ lit. ‘opposite’.
²⁹ So GRA. V and H omit ‘inserts in … place of’.
³⁰ The one omitted and the one erased.
³¹ The two erased and the one omitted.
³² In order to insert more than one missing line. The erasure of one or two lines is sufficient for the insertion of the omitted line without undue pressure on the space between the lines; but the treatment of more than one line in this manner would disfigure the entire column (N.Y.).


###### Tractate Soferim 5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/5:9)


אין עושין שמע הלל מגלה לתינוקות ר׳ יהודה מתיר מן בראשית עד המבול מן ויקרא עד ויהי ביום השמיני ושאר כל הספרים אסור (אם עתיד הוא להוסיף עליו מותר):


It is not permissible to make a scroll of the *Shema‘* or of the *Hallel* for children,³³ but if the scribe intends to add to it, it is permissible.³⁴ R. Judah permits the writing of a scroll from *In the beginning*³⁵ to the story of the flood,³⁶ and from the beginning of Leviticus to *And it came to pass on the eighth day;*³⁷ but in the case of all the other books [of Scripture the writing of extracts is] forbidden.³⁸

footnotes:
³³ Extracts from Scripture may not be made for study by children.
³⁴ From ‘but if’ to ‘permissible’ is an addition by GRA.
³⁵ Gen. 1, 1.
³⁶ i.e. to Gen. 6, 8. In each instance the extract is a connected theme.
³⁷ Lev. 9, 1.
³⁸ Only in the case of the Pentateuch were the two extracts mentioned allowed to be written for the instruction of children. V adds here, ‘if he intends to add to it, it is permitted’.


###### Tractate Soferim 5:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/5:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/5:10)


אלו שמות הנחלקין בית אל בית און באר שבע צפנת פענח פוטי פרע בן אוני ידיד יה הללו יה (וחרה אף וחרה אפי) רבי יוסי אומר אין נחלקין אבל הכל שוין שאין חולקין עמיאל עמינדב צוריאל צורישדי:


The following names are to be divided:³⁹ Beth-el,⁴⁰ Beth-’awen,⁴¹ Beer-sheba,⁴² Ẓofenath-pa’neaḥ, [37*b*] Poṭi-fera,⁴³ XLI, 45. E.V. *Zaphenathpaneah* and *Poti-phera*. Ben-’oni,⁴⁴ XXXV, 18. Yedid-yah,⁴⁵. Halelu-yah.⁴⁶ [*of the Lord*] *was kindled, My wrath shall wax hot* (Ex. 22, 23; Num. 11, 10). R. Jose says: These must not be divided.⁴⁷ All, however, agree that there must be no breaking up of ‘Ammi’el,⁴⁸ ‘Amminadab,⁴⁹ 23). Ẓuri’el,⁵⁰ Ẓurishaddai.⁵¹

footnotes:
³⁹ Although each represents one name, it is to be written as two words.
⁴⁰ e.g. Gen. 12, 8.
⁴¹ Hos. 4, 15, X, 15.
⁴² E.g. Gen. 21, 31.
⁴³ ibid.
⁴⁴ ibid.
⁴⁵ 2 Sam. 12, 25. E.V. Jedidiah
⁴⁶ E.g. Ps. 111, 1, ‘praise ye the Lord’. E.V. prints it as one word. V and H add: וחרה אף וחרה אפי, and the anger
⁴⁷ But written as one word.
⁴⁸ A name meaning ‘God is with me’ (e.g. Num. 13, 12). Another reading is עזיאל, ‘God is my strength’ (e.g. Ex. 6, 18).
⁴⁹ A name meaning ‘my kinsman is noble’ (e.g. ibid.
⁵⁰ A name meaning ‘my Rock is God’ (Num. 3, 35).
⁵¹ A name meaning ‘my Rock is Shaddai’ (cf. Num. 1, 6).


###### Tractate Soferim 5:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/5:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/5:11)


אלו שמות שאין נחלקין ישראל געתם פוטיפר נבוכדנצר (עמיאל צוריאל צורישדי):


The following names may not be broken up: Yisra’el, Ga’tham,⁵². Poṭifar, Nebukadneẓẓar.⁵³

footnotes:
⁵² A name occurring in Gen. 36, 11, 16, 1 Chron. 1, 36. E.V. Gatam
⁵³ V and H unnecessarily repeat צמיאל, עוריאל, צורישדי.


###### Tractate Soferim 5:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/5:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/5:12)


הכותב את השם על בשרו לא ירחוץ ולא יסוך ר׳ יוסי מתיר ובלבד שלא ישפשף:


If a person writes a divine name on his body, he must neither bathe nor anoint himself⁵⁴ (nor stand in an unclean place.⁵⁵ If he must perform an obligatory immersion,⁵⁶ he should wind a reed about it,⁵⁷ descend and perform an immersion. R. Jose said: He may at all times descend and perform an immersion in the ordinary way)⁵⁸ provided he does not rub it off.

footnotes:
⁵⁴ So that the name be not washed away.
⁵⁵ Out of respect.
⁵⁶ As, e.g., after a period of ritual impurity.
⁵⁷ To prevent the name from coming into contact with the water which might wash it away.
⁵⁸ The words in brackets are inserted by GRA who follows Shab. 120b (Sonc. ed., p. 597). V and H read: R. Jose permits, provided, etc.


###### Tractate Soferim 5:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/5:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/5:13)


על קרן הפרה ועל כרעי המיטה גוררו וגונזה על האבן שומטה וגונזה:


[If one wrote a divine name] on the horn of a cow or on the legs of a bed,⁵⁹ he scrapes it off⁶⁰ and stores it away. [If he wrote it] on a stone, he detaches it and stores it away.⁶¹

footnotes:
⁵⁹ For magical purposes.
⁶⁰ [The correct reading is perhaps גודדד for גוררד, ‘he chops off (the horn or leg)’.]
⁶¹ From respect for the divine name.


###### Tractate Soferim 5:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/5:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/5:14)


ספר שחותך התפר שלו אחת חוזר ויתפור שנית חוזר ויתפור שלישית לא יתפור המחתך בספרים חדשים מותר בישנים [אסור] מקק הספרים מקק התפילין מקק מטפחות הספרים הרי אלו יגנוזו:


If the seam of a sacred scroll was broken for the first time,⁶² it may be sewn up again;⁶³ if a second time, it may be sewn up again; if a third time, it must not be sewn up again.⁶⁴ If one cuts off a strip of a new scroll,⁶⁵ it may [be used for ordinary purposes],⁶⁶ but if it is cut off an old scroll,⁶⁷ [its use is] forbidden. Worn-out scrolls, phylacteries and wraps of scrolls must be stored away.⁶⁸

footnotes:
⁶² lit. ‘one’.
⁶³ On the undamaged part of the sheet, near the writing, although the margins between the columns are thereby reduced.
⁶⁴ Because the margins would become too small.
⁶⁵ lit. ‘scrolls’.
⁶⁶ Being new, the scroll had not yet been consecrated.
⁶⁷ Which has been consecrated by the script.
⁶⁸ Having once been consecrated, they may no longer be used for ordinary purposes.


###### Tractate Soferim 5:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/5:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/5:15)


מעשה בר' חלפתא שהלך לפני רבן גמליאל ומצאו יושב על שולחנו ובידו תרגום של איוב והיה קורא בו א"ל רבי תרשיני לומר מה שראו עיני אמר לו אמור צופה הייתי ברבן גמליאל הזקן אבי (אמך) [אביך] שהיה יושב על גב הבנין בהר הבית והביאו לפניו תרגום של איוב ואמר (לבניו) [לבנאי] סלק את השורה ושקע זה תחתיו ר׳ אומר ב' תשובות בדבר אחד טיט בהר הבית מנין [ועוד] שאינו רשאי לאבדן ביד אבל לא יניחן במקום הטינוף אלא במקום התורפה והן מרקיבין מאליהן:


It is related of R. Ḥalafta that, when he visited Rabban Gamaliel, he found him sitting at his table with the Targum of Job⁶⁹ in his hand and reading it. He said to him, ‘Master, will you permit me to tell you what my eyes saw?’ ‘Tell me,’ he replied. ‘I was watching Rabban Gamaliel the Elder, your father’s⁷⁰ father, while he was sitting at the side of a building [in course of construction] on the Temple Mount, when a Targum of Job was brought to him. “Remove a course of stones,” he ordered the builder,⁷¹ “and bury this under it.” ’ R. Jose b. R. Judah said: They overturned a tub of mortar upon it.⁷² Rabbi said: There are two objections to the statement—firstly, how could mortar⁷³ be available on the Temple Mount?⁷⁴ Further, it is not permissible to destroy such writings⁷⁵ with one’s own hands! [They should not be deposited in an unclean place] but in a deserted spot where they decay of their own accord.⁷⁶

footnotes:
⁶⁹ This must have been an earlier work than the extant Targum. [The anecdote is related in Shab. 115a (Sonc. ed., p. 565) with some variants.]
⁷⁰ V rightly corrects ‘mother’s’ to ‘father’s’.
⁷¹ V incorrectly ‘his children’.
⁷² This sentence is added by GRA from Shab. 115a.
⁷³ Which is composed of earth and water.
⁷⁴ Where only a mixture of sand and lime was used.
⁷⁵ Books like the Targum of Job whose use was forbidden on account of their exclusion from the Canon.
⁷⁶ The objection to a written, as against an oral, Targum at the time was the fear that the same sanctity might be attached to it as to the Hebrew text.


###### Tractate Soferim 5:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/5:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/5:16)


המשאיל ספר תורה לחבירו אינו רשאי להשאילה לאחר אם יודע לקרות בו קורא אם בשביל לפתחו הרי זה אסור סומכוס אומר חדש גוללו אחד לשלשים יום א׳ חדש וא' ישן אחד לשנים עשר חדש:


If one lends a Torah scroll to his friend, the latter may not lend it to another person. If one deposits a Torah scroll with his friend, the latter must unroll it once in twelve months.⁷⁷ If he is capable of reading it, he may do so,⁷⁸ but if he unrolled it for his own benefit,⁷⁹ reading it is forbidden. Symmachos said: He unrolls a new scroll once in thirty days and an old one once in twelve months. R. Eliezer b. Jacob says:⁸⁰ Both a new and an old scroll [should be unrolled] once in twelve months.⁸¹

footnotes:
⁷⁷ This sentence is inserted by GRA following B.M. 29b (Sonc. ed., p. 182).
⁷⁸ While the scroll is being unrolled in accordance with this Rule.
⁷⁹ So corrected by GRA.
⁸⁰ From ‘and an old’ to ‘says’ is added by GRA from B.M. 29b.
⁸¹ In the course of the discussion in B.M. 29b this is amended to ‘thirty days’ to differentiate the opinion of R. Eliezer from that of the first authority who is quoted above.


###### Tractate Soferim 5:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/5:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/5:17)


כל השלמות האמורים בשיר השירים כולם קודש חוץ מאחד שהוא חול ואיזהו (ש"ה ג) הנה מטתו שלשלמה וי"א (שם ח) האלף לך שלמה:


All occurrences of the name Solomon in the Song of Songs are sacred⁸² except one which is secular. Which one is it? *Behold, it is the litter of Solomon*.⁸³ Others say: *Thou, O Solomon, shalt have the thousand*.⁸⁴ VIII, 12.

footnotes:
⁸² Referring to God in the Rabbinical interpretation of the Book.
⁸³ Cant. 3, 7.
⁸⁴ ibid.


###### Tractate Soferim 5:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/5:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/5:18)


כל מלכיא האמורים בדניאל כולם חול חוץ מאחד שהוא קודש ואיזהו זה (דניאל ב׳:ל״ו-ל״ז) מלכא מלך מלכיא די אלה שמיא מלכותא הסנא ותקפא ויקרא יהב לך וי"א (שם ד) [מרי] חלמא לשנאך ופשריה לערך:


All references to ‘kings’ in Daniel are secular except one which is sacred. Which one is it? *O king unto whom the King*⁸⁵ *of kings, the God of heaven, hath given the kingdom, the power, and the strength, and the glory*.⁸⁶, etc. Others say: *My Lord*,⁸⁷ *the dream be to them that hate thee, and the interpretation thereof to thine adversaries*.⁸⁸ IV, 16.

footnotes:
⁸⁵ Understood as referring to God.
⁸⁶ Dan. 2, 37. E.V. O king, king of kings, unto whom
⁸⁷ Interpreted as an invocation to God in Shebu. 35b (Sonc. ed., p. 207).
⁸⁸ ibid.



## Chapter 6

###### Tractate Soferim 6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/6:1)


הכותב צריך לעשות שיעור בפתיחה של (במדבר י׳:ל״ה) ויהי בנסוע הארון מלמעלה ומלמטה שהוא ספר בפני עצמו וי"א שמקומו בנסיעת דגלים:


A scribe must provide a distinguishing mark¹. for the section² beginning *And it came to pass when the ark set forward*,³ 35f. both at its beginning and at its end, because it is a book on its own. Others maintain that its proper place is in the section of the setting forward of the standards.⁴

footnotes:
¹ The traditional mark is an inverted nun
² So GRA. V reads ‘a prescribed space at the opening’.
³ Num. 10, 35. The section consists of the two verses, ibid.
⁴ Num. 2.


###### Tractate Soferim 6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/6:2)


(הכותב צריך לעשות שיעור בפתיחה של ויהי העם כמתאוננים מלמעלה ומלמטה שהוא ספר בפני עצמו וי"א שמקומו בנסיעת דגלים):


⁵ X, 35f, is followed by the beginning of *And the people were as murmurers* (*ibid.* XI, 1); and partly incorrect since no mark is inserted at the end of the latter section. The entire Rule is to be deleted according to GRA. The scribe must leave a prescribed space at the opening of the section which begins *And the people were as murmurers;*⁶ XI, 1. [and not only] at its beginning [but also] at its end, because it is a book on its own. Others hold that its proper place is in the section of the setting forward of the standards.

footnotes:
⁵ This Rule is partly a repetition of the preceding, since the end of the section, ibid.
⁶ ibid.


###### Tractate Soferim 6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/6:3)


עשר נקודות בתורה (בראשית ט״ז:ה׳) ישפוט ה׳ ביני וביניך יו"ד שביניך נקוד (שם יח) ויאמרו אליו אי"ו נקוד (שם יט) ולא ידע בשכבה ובקומה וי"ו נקוד שבקומה (שם לג) ויפול על צוארו וישקהו כולו נקוד (שם לז) וילכו אחיו לרעות את צאן אביהם את נקוד (במדבר ג׳:ל״ט) אשר פקד משה ואהרן אהרן נקוד (שם ט) או בדרך רחוקה ה"י נקוד (שם כא) ונשים עד נפח אשר רי"ש נקוד (שם כט) ועשרון עשרון שבחג בי"ט הראשון וי"ו שבעשרון השני נקוד (דברים כ״ט:כ״ח) הנסתרות [לה׳ אלהינו] והנגלות לנו ולבנינו עד עולם עיין שבעד נקוד:


Ten [letters or groups of letters] in the Torah are marked by dots:⁷ In *the Lord judge between me and thee*⁸ there is a dot on the *beth*⁹’. of *ubeneka* (and between thee). In *and they said unto him*,¹⁰ XVIII, 9. there are dots on *alef-yod-waw* of *’elaw* (unto him). In *and he knew not when she lay down, nor when she arose*,¹¹ XIX, 33. the [second] *waw* in *ubekumah* (nor when she arose) is marked with a dot. In *and kissed him*,¹² XXXIII, 4. the entire word *wayyishshaḳehu* is marked by dots. In *and his brethren went to feed their father’s flock*,¹³ XXXVII, 12. the letters of *’eth* (the mark of the defined accusative) are dotted. In *whom Moses and Aaron numbered*¹⁴ the word *Aaron* is marked with dots. In *or be in a journey afar off*,¹⁵ IX, 10. the *he* in *reḥoḳah* (afar off) has a dot. In *and we have laid waste even unto Nopha, which*¹⁶ XXI, 30. the *resh* in *’asher* (which) has a dot. In *and a several tenth part*,¹⁷ XXIX, 15. in the section dealing with the first day of the festival of Tabernacles, the second *waw* in *we’issaron* (and a tenth part) has a dot. In *the secret things belong unto the Lord our God; but the things that are revealed belong unto us and to our children for* ever,¹⁸ in עד’. Cf. Sanh. 43b (Sonc. ed., p. 285, n. 4) and ARN above, p. 166.] the *‘ayin* in *‘ad* (for) has a dot.

footnotes:
⁷ The dots are placed above the letters. Cf. above, ARN XXXIV, p. 165 where the Rabbinical explanations are given.
⁸ Gen. 16, 5.
⁹ So GRA. V and H ‘the yod
¹⁰ ibid.
¹¹ ibid.
¹² ibid.
¹³ ibid.
¹⁴ Num. 3, 39.
¹⁵ ibid.
¹⁶ ibid.
¹⁷ ibid.
¹⁸ Deut. 29, 28. [H more correctly, ‘unto us and to our children and the ‘ayin


###### Tractate Soferim 6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/6:4)


א"ר שמעון בן לקיש שלשה ספרים נמצאו בעזרה ספר מעונה ספר זאטוטי ספר היא באחד מצאו כתוב מעון ובשנים כתוב מעונה אלהי קדם (שם לג) וקיימו שנים ובטלו אחד באחד מצאו כתוב וישלח אל זאטוטי בני ישראל ובשנים מצאו כתוב וישלח את נערי בני ישראל (שמות כ״ד:ה׳) וקיימו שנים ובטלו א׳ באחד כתוב אחד עשר הוא ובשנים מצאו כתוב אחד עשר היא וקיימו שנים ובטלו אחד:


R. Simeon b. Laḳish said: Three scrolls of the Torah were found in the Temple court:¹⁹ the *Ma‘on*²⁰ scroll, the *Za’aṭuṭë* scroll, and the *Hu’* scroll. In one of these they found the expression of *ma‘on*,²¹ and in the other two it was written, *The eternal God is me‘onah*²² (a dwelling place), so they adopted the reading of the two scrolls and discarded that of the one scroll. In another of the scrolls they found it written, *And he sent the za‘aṭuṭë* (nobles) *of the children of Israel*,²³ and in the other two they found written *And he sent na‘arë* (the young men of) *the children of Israel*, so they retained the reading of the two and abandoned that of the one. In one of the scrolls *hu*’²⁴, third person fem. sing. pronoun. was written eleven times, but in the other two *hi*’²⁵ was written eleven times, so they adopted the reading of the two and discarded that of the one.

footnotes:
¹⁹ Presumably when the text of Scripture was about to be fixed.
²⁰ So GRA. V has מענה. This and the following two names were derived from the peculiar word which distinguished each scroll.
²¹ Meaning ‘dwelling place’, the noun being in the masculine form.
²² Deut. 33, 27, the same noun in the feminine.
²³ Ex. 24, 5.
²⁴ The third person sing. masc. which is used in the Pentateuch for hi’
²⁵ The correct feminine form, being exceptions to the usual written form. A list of these is given in ARN XXXIV, above p. 167.


###### Tractate Soferim 6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/6:5)


שלשה לא כותבים בלמ"ד אל"ף וקוראים בלמ"ד וי"ו ואלו הן אשר לא כרעים ממעל לרגליו (ויקרא י״א:כ״א) אשר לא חומה (שם כה) אשר לא (יגיד) [יעדה] (שמות כא):


Three expressions of *lo’* are written *lamed-alef*²⁶ but read as *lamed-waw*.²⁷ They are: *’asher lo’*²⁸ *jointed legs above their feet;*²⁹ *’asher lo’ ḥomah;*³⁰ XXV, 30, *lamed-alef*, meaning ‘which has no wall’ but read as *lamed-waw* ‘which has a wall’. E.V. *in a walled city*. and *’asher lo’ ye‘adah*.³¹ means ‘who hath not espoused her’, but the reading with *lamed-waw* means, as E.V., *who hath espoused her to himself*.

footnotes:
²⁶ Meaning ‘not’.
²⁷ Which means ‘to him, has’.
²⁸ לא, lit. ‘which not’ but read לו ‘which has’.
²⁹ Lev. 11, 21.
³⁰ ibid.
³¹ Ex. 21, 8. The written form with lamed-alef


###### Tractate Soferim 6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/6:6)


ואלו הן של נביאים ושל כתובים תרבו חושי אמר (לו) הרבית צרתם יקטלני אחריש כצפור מרעהו זרובבל יצרו דעו יאסף שם ודברו:


The following [occurrences of *lamed-alef* (not)] which have to be read as *lamed-waw* (to, by, has) are found in the Prophets and Hagiographa: *multiply;*³² according to the *ḳerë*, but the *kethib* reads ‘and actions are not weighed’. *Hushai;*³³ is *his will I be* and the *kethib* ‘I will not be’. *said;*³⁴ is *say unto him, thou shalt surely recover*, and the *kethib* ‘say, thou shalt not surely recover’. *thou hast multiplied;*³⁵ is *Thou hast increased their joy*, and the *kethib* ‘Thou hast not increased joy’. *their affliction;*³⁶ is *In all their affliction He was afflicted* (lit. there was affliction to Him) and the *kethib* ‘in all their affliction He was not an adversary’. *He slay me;*³⁷ is *yet will I trust Him*, the *kethib* ‘I have nothing to hope for’. *I keep silence;*³⁸ is *would I keep silence* (to him), the *kethib* ‘I would not keep silence’. *as the* (*wandering*) *sparrow;*³⁹ is *shall come home* (to him), the *kethib* ‘shall not come home’. *his friends;*⁴⁰ is *they turn against him*, the *kethib* ‘they do not turn’. *Zerubbabel;*⁴¹ is *we do sacrifice unto Him*, the *kethib* ‘we do not sacrifice’. *were fashioned;*⁴² is ‘and for it there was one among them’, the *kethib when as yet there was none of them*. In this instance E.V. follows the *kethib*. *know ye;*⁴³ is *we are His*, the *kethib* ‘and not we (have made) ourselves’. *be gathered;*⁴⁴ is *be gathered unto Him*, the *kethib* ‘be not gathered’. *name*,⁴⁵ is *and had a name among the three*, the *kethib* ‘and not a name among the three’. [this last also] in the corresponding passage.⁴⁶ is both written and read. [More probably ודבר in V is a misreading of *daleth* signifying ‘four’ which should be attached to the beginning of the next Rule.] [38*a*]

## Chapter 7

###### Tractate Soferim 7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/7:1)


אלו כתובין חסרון וי"ו וקוראין בוי"ו ויעלהו אמרו ויאמרו וידברו (ויקחו) [יקחו] ויתנהו ואכלו יחדיו (יהללו) [וחללהו] ויעלו (שמו) [שויו] וקבלו מלכיה וחילופיהן רגלי שמטוה ויצוה נכחו שער העין ויקראו לו ויקהל תפס:


The following words are written without a *waw*,¹ but are to be read with the *waw: and offered it;*² *said;*³ 19. Here and in the other verses which are cited the *kethib* is the singular and the *ḳerë* the plural. *and* [*they*] *said;*⁴ XII, 10. *and they spoke;*⁵ *shall they take away;*⁶ *and let them deliver;*⁷ XXII, 5. *and eat;*⁸ *together;*⁹ to be read for *yaḥad*. *and they shall profane it;*¹⁰ *and they offered;*¹¹ *was made;*¹² *and took upon them;*¹³ *Hananiah;*¹⁴ indicates Neh. 3, 30 where the text omits the *waw* at the end of *’aḥaraw* (*after him*). *Malchijah*.¹⁵ 31 where the *waw* at the end of *’aḥaraw* is also omitted. The reverse of this [occurs in the following words]:¹⁶ is written but not read. *my feet;*¹⁷ is ‘his feet’. [Although not included here, in the preceding verse *my way is* ‘his way’ in the *kethib*.] *throw her down;*¹⁸ is due to dittography and superfluous.] *and … commanded;*¹⁹ XVI, 15. The *kethib* means ‘commanded him’. *straight before him;*²⁰ is written as plural but to be read as singular. *the fountain gate;*²¹ is ‘they set up’ and the *ḳerë* (he) *set up*. *and called him;*²² and singular in the *ḳerë*. *and he assembled;*²³ 21. Here the reference is to the first verb *was come* which is written as plural but to be read as singular. *took*.²⁴ with the singular to be read for the plural in the text.

אלו כותבין [בה"א] ולא קוראין וארא ואביא הארי דשמואל (מוצא) [נמצא] ותרא הזאת רע הרע בא ובא וקו וקו (הרע) אוהבי ורע יעש פקח אריאל (צור) [ציד] וחלופן (וארכה הגונה) [וארבה הגגה] והמה יהיה והיתה (ונראה) (והמה יהיה) [הנה] שתול גדליהו ותעגבה המדה ואכבדה שלשה (דממה) [זממה] טעמה קומי זכור השיבנו  הגדתה קללת (שמתה) [שתה] עשית נבהלה פתוחיה תזנותיה שבנה (אבלה) [אגלה] (שקטה) [שכת] ויראה:


The following are written with a *he* which is not pronounced: *when I saw;*²⁵ and read *wa’erë’*. *and I brought;*²⁶ XXIV, 8, written *wa’abih* and read *wa’abi*. *the lion*²⁷ and read *ha’ari*. in the Book of Samuel;²⁸ is both written and read. *was he found;*²⁹ but the reading is *nimẓa’*. *and … saw;*³⁰ III, 7, written *wattir’eh* and read *watter’ë’*. *this* [*city*];³¹ XXVI, 6, written *hazzothah* and read *hazzoth*. *evil;*³² and read *ra‘*. [*the*] *evil;*³³ and read *hara‘*. *gone down;*³⁴ XV, 9, written *ba’ah* and read *ba’*. *and he shall come;*³⁵ XLIII, 11, written *uba’ah* and read *uba’*. *and a line;*³⁶ and read *weḳaw*. *and a line;*³⁷ *thoroughly;*³⁸. Ps. 51, 4, written *harbeh* and read *hereb*. *them that love me;*³⁹ and read *’ohabai*. *and … friend;*⁴⁰ XXVII, 10, written were‘eh and read *were‘a*. *deal;*⁴¹ and read *ya‘as*. *open;*⁴² and read *peḳaḥ*. *a lion;*⁴³ and read *’ari*. V incorrectly connects this with the next word joining them together as one. *these;*⁴⁴ and read *’el*. *venison*.⁴⁵. Gen. 27, 3, written *ẓedah* and read *ẓayid*.
The reverse is the case with the following:⁴⁶ is not pronounced, but the following are spelt without a *he* which is to be pronounced. *and multiplied;*⁴⁷ and read *wa’arbeh*. *on the housetop;*⁴⁸ and read *haggagah*. *and they;*⁴⁹ and read *wehemmah*. *be he;*⁵⁰ and read *yihyeh*. *and … shal be;*⁵¹ and read *wehayethah*. *and behold;*⁵² and read *wenireh*. *behold;*⁵³ LIV, 16, written *hen* and read *hinneh*. *planted;*⁵⁴ (*shall … see*) which is spelt without the final *hei*. *Gedaliah;*⁵⁵ XL, 16, referring to *ta‘aseh* (*do*) which is without the final *hei*. *and … she doted;*⁵⁶ and read *watta‘gebah*. *the measure;*⁵⁷ XLV, 3, where *ḥamesh* (*five*) is written and *ḥamishshah* read. *and I shall be glorified;*⁵⁸ and read *we’ekkabedah*. *three;*⁵⁹ (*yea, four*) is written and *we’arba‘ah* read. *she considereth;*⁶⁰ XXXI, 16, *naṭa‘* (*planteth*) is written and *naṭ‘ah* read. *she perceiveth;*⁶¹ 18, *ballayil* (*by night*) is written and *ballayelah* read. *arise;*⁶² *remember;*⁶³ V, 1, *habbeṭ* (*behold*) is written and *habbiṭah* read. *turn Thou us;*⁶⁴ 21, *wenashub* (*and we shall be turned*) is written and *wenashubah* read. *thou hast declared;*⁶⁵ is written and *we’attah* read. *thou … hast cursed;*⁶⁶ *Thou hast set;*⁶⁷ and read *shattah*. *Thou hast made;*⁶⁸ and read *’attah*. *is sore affrighted;*⁶⁹ *the carved work thereof;*⁷⁰ LXXIV, 6, written *we‘at* and read *we‘attah*. *with her harlotries;*⁷¹ and read *‘attah*. *turn back;*⁷² (*go your way*) omits the final *hei*. *disclose;*⁷³ IV, 4, where *we’eda‘* (*that I may know*) is written and read *we’ede‘ah*. *Thou* [*hast*] *made an hedge;*⁷⁴ (*Thou*) is written and *’attah* read. *and* [*he*] *saw*.⁷⁵ XLII, 16, written *wayyar* and read *wayyireh*.

read *wetilon*. *Jehiel;*¹²⁹ and read *Jeḥi’el*. *and prepare ye;*¹³⁰ XXXV, 4, written *wehikkonu* and read *wehakkinu*. *thy bosom;*¹³¹ and read *ḥeḳeka*. *a side-structure;*¹³² and read *yaẓi‘a*. *Jair;*¹³³ and read *Ya‘ir*. *alienate;*¹³⁴ and read *ya‘abir*. *Jeiel;*¹³⁵ and read *Je‘i’el*. *they wander up and down;*¹³⁶ and read *yeni‘un*. *let … cover them;*¹³⁷ CXL, 10, written *yekassumo* and read *yekassemo*. *they cause … to fall;*¹³⁸ and read *yakshilu*. *to strive;*¹³⁹ and read *larib*. *singing;*¹⁴⁰ and read *lashir*. *Laish;*¹⁴¹ and read *Layish*. *for fishers;*¹⁴² and read *ledayyagim*. *dross;*¹⁴³ and read *lesig*. *for a spoil;*¹⁴⁴ and read *limshissah*. *their furrows;*¹⁴⁵ and read *lema‘anitham*. *Mephaath;*¹⁴⁶ and read *mepa‘ath*. *from Naioth;*¹⁴⁷ and read *minnayoth*. *stretched-forth;*¹⁴⁸ and read *neṭioth*. *fruit;*¹⁴⁹ LVII, 19, written *nob* and read *nib*. *Nebai;*¹⁵⁰ and read *Nebai*. *Nephishesim;*¹⁵¹ VII, 52, written *Nefushesim* and read *Nefishesim*. *leave;*¹⁵² and read *sim*. *ready dressed;*¹⁵³ and read *‘asiyoth*. *Ephai;*¹⁵⁴ and read *‘ephai*. *Ephrain;*¹⁵⁵ and read *‘Efrain*. V incorrectly reads *‘Abarim*. the second¹⁵⁶ in Esth. 3, 14. *ready;*¹⁵⁷ and read *‘athidim*. *their lads;*¹⁵⁸ and read *ẓe‘irehem*. *her little ones;*¹⁵⁹ XLVIII, 4, written *ẓe‘oreha* and read *ẓe‘ireha*. *dung*.¹⁶⁰ and read *ẓefi‘ë*. ## Chapter 8

###### Tractate Soferim 8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/8:1)


אלו הן חלופי שירות תהלים בשירות שמואל וידבר ומפלטי לי אלי צורי משגבי ומנוסי ומאויבי כי אפפוני משברי נחלי סבבוני אקרא וישמע ויתגעש מוסדות השמים. וירא. וישת סוכות. חשרת. בערו גחלי. ירעם מן שמים חצים ויהום אפיקי מים ויגלו בגערת אפו משנאי משען ויוצא אותי בצדקתי אסור ממנה ואהיה לו ואשתמרה כצדקתי כבורי גבור תתבר תתפל ואת ועיניך על רמים תשפיל ואלדי באלדי. מי אל מבלעדי. מעוזי. ויתר ונחת וענותך תחתני. ארדפה. ואשמידם. ואכלם ואמחצם. יקומון ויפלו. ותזרני. תחתני. משנאי ואצמיתם ישעו. אל ה׳. כעפר ארץ. אדיקם ארקעם ותפלטני. עמי (תשימני) [תשמרני] יתכחשו לי. לשמוע. ויחגרו (ממסגרותיהם) [ממסגרותם] צור. ומוריד. תחתני. ומוציאי. ומקמי. חמסים. ה׳ ראשון. אומר מגדול:


Variants¹ between the poem² of Psalm [XVIII] and Samuel³ are the following: *and … spoke;*⁴, etc. *and my deliverer;*⁵ by a suffix and pronoun; Ps. 3 omits the pronoun. *my God, my rock;*⁶. *my high tower, and my refuge;*⁷. *from mine enemies;*⁸ is the prefix *mem*, in Ps. 4 the preposition *min*. *for … compassed me;*⁹ is wanting. *the waves of;*¹⁰. *the floods of;*¹¹. *compassed me;*¹², in Ps. 6 *sebabuni*. The meaning is the same. *I called;*¹³. *and … He heard;*¹⁴. *then … did shake;*¹⁵ in Ps. 8 *wattig‘ash*. The meaning is practically the same. *the foundations of heaven;*¹⁶. *yea, He was seen;*¹⁷. *and He made;*¹⁸. *pavilions;*¹⁹. *gathering of:*²⁰. *coals of fire flamed forth;*²¹. *thundered from heaven;*²². *arrows;*²³. *and discomfited them;*²⁴ (*ḳerë*), but the *kethib* is the same as in Ps. 15 *wayyehummem*. *the channels of waters;*²⁵. *and … were laid bare;*²⁶ is wanting. *by the rebuke of the Lord;*²⁷. *His nostrils;*²⁸. *from them that hated me;*²⁹. *a stay;*³⁰ in Ps. 19 *lemish‘an*. The sense is the same. *He brought me forth also;*³¹ *according*³² *to my righteousness;*³³, in Ps. 21 *keẓidḳi*. The nouns are practically synonymous. *I did not depart from them;*³⁴. *and I was;*³⁵ Ps. 24 *wa’ehi* is a shortened form of the same verb. *toward Him;*³⁶. *and I kept myself;*³⁷ at the end. *according to my righteousness;*³⁸ Ps. 25 *keẓidḳi*. *according to my cleanness;*³⁹. *man;*⁴⁰ (lit. mighty man), in Ps. 26 *gebar* (*man*). *Thou dost show Thyself pure;*⁴¹, in Ps. 27 *tithbarar*. *Thou dost show Thyself subtle;*⁴² in Ps. 27 *tithpattal*. *and … Thou dost save;*⁴³. *but Thine eyes are upon the haughty, that Thou mayest humble them;*⁴⁴. *and the Lord;*⁴⁵. *by my God;*⁴⁶. *who is God;*⁴⁷, Ps. 32 *’elo’ah*. *save;*⁴⁸. A different preposition is used. V *ba‘adi* is an error for *mibbal‘adë*. *my … fortress;*⁴⁹. *who letteth … go forth;*⁵⁰. The former reads *wayyater*, the latter *wayyitten*. *do bend;*⁵¹, Ps. 35 *weniḥathah*, with the same sense. *and Thy condescension;*⁵² Ps. 36 *we‘anwatheka*. *under me;*⁵³, Ps. 37 *taḥtai*. *I have pursued;*⁵⁴, Ps. 38 *’erdof*. *and destroyed them;*⁵⁵. *and I have consumed them;*⁵⁶ *and smitten them;*⁵⁷. *that they cannot arise;*⁵⁸. *yea, they are fallen;*⁵⁹. *for Thou hast girded me;*⁶⁰ from the verb. *under me;*⁶¹, Ps. 40 *taḥtai*. *yea, them that hate me, that I might cut them off;*⁶². *they looked;*⁶³. *even unto the Lord;*⁶⁴ is *’el*, in Ps. 42 *‘al*. *as the dust of the earth;*⁶⁵. *I did stamp them … I did tread them down;*⁶⁶. *Thou also hast delivered me … my people. Thou hast kept me;*⁶⁷. *dwindle away … as soon as they hear of me;*⁶⁸. In Sam. the Heb. for *hear* and *dwindle away* is *lishmoa‘* and *yithkaḥashu*, in Ps. *leshema‘* and *yekaḥashu*. *and come halting out of their close places;*⁶⁹. The Heb. for *out of their close places* is *mimmisgerotham* in Sam. and *mimmisgerothehem* in Ps. There is no difference in the Heb. verb despite the different translation. *my Rock of salvation;*⁷⁰ in the second part of the verse. *and bringeth down … under me;*⁷¹. There is also a difference in the Heb. for *under me* as in verses 37, 40. *and that bringeth me forth … yea … above them that rise up against me … violent;*⁷². The Heb. for *above me* and *violent* in Sam. is *umiḳḳamai* and *ḥamasim;* in Ps. *min ḳammai* and *ḥamas*. the Tetragrammaton is placed first;⁷³, in Ps. 50 it follows it. *I will sing praises;*⁷⁴, in Ps. 50 *’azammerah*. ‘Omer’ in V is a printer’s error. *a tower of salvation*.⁷⁵. The Heb. in the former is *migdol*, in the latter *magdil*.

###### Tractate Soferim 8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/8:2)


אלו הם חלופי ישעיה (הקוראים) במלכים ובארבע חזקיה רב סריס אל המלך ירושלים [ויעלו] ויבואו [ירושלים] ויעלו [ויבואו] ויעמדו (ירושלים) ויקראו (אלהים) [אל המלך] אלהם אמרת עתה לך תאמרון בירושלים את מלך עתה על המקום [הזה] להשחיתו על הארץ בן חלקיהו עמנו אליהם העל וידבר דבר ואל תשמעו כי יסית (ההיכל הנענועה) [ההצל הצילו הנע ועוה] כי הצילו הארצות והחרישו העם דברי רבשקה ושבנא הנביא קדמיית' את כל דברי להם ישעיה לארצו אל תרהקה הנה יצא (יצא) וישב את אשר ה׳ אלהי שמעתי (אמר לקדוש) [אל קדוש] מלאכיך מבחור ואבואה מלון זרים למימי להשות (ויחבישו) [ויבשו] ושדפה סחיש יבא אל העיר ויהי בלילה [ההוא] ויך שמונים את פניו לאמר אנא ובלבב שוב נגיד עמי רופא לך והספתי למעני קחו ויקחו וישימו ויהי אל ישעיהו כי ירפא זה לך כי יעשה את הדבר הלך (בארץ) [בראדך] כי שמע חזקיהו וישמע את כל שמן בית כליו באו דבר ה׳ בבלה הלא אם :


The following are variants between Isaiah⁷⁶ and the text of Kings:⁷⁷ *Now in the four*[*teenth*];⁷⁸[*teenth*]. *Hezekiah;*⁷⁹ 14-16 the name is spelt *Ḥizḳiyyah* instead of *Ḥizḳiyyahu*. *Rab-saris … to king Hezehiah … unto Jerusalem. And they went up and came to Jerusalem. And when they were come up, they came and stood by*.⁸⁰ is lacking, the order of the words is *to Jerusalem unto the king*. The clause *and they went up … they came* is lacking, and *stood* is in the singular but the plural in Kings. *And when they had called to the king … unto them*.⁸¹ is lacking in Isa. 3 which has *unto them* for *unto him*. *Sayest thou*⁸². *now … thou trustest;*⁸³ is lacking, as is also *leka* (lit. thou trusteth for thyself), compared with Kings 21. *ye say … in Jerusalem;*⁸⁴ in Kings 22, Isa. 7 has *thou say* and omits *in Jerusalem*. *with … king;*⁸⁵ is *’eth melek;* in Isa. 8 it is *’eth hammelek*. *now … against this place … against this land and destroy it;*⁸⁶ in Kings 25, Isa. 10 reads *and … now*. In *against this land* the preposition is *‘al* for *’el*. *the son of Hilkiah … with us;*⁸⁷ is lacking, and *to us* occurs instead of *with us*. *unto them … hath … to;*⁸⁸ is omitted, and *hath … to* is *ha‘al* for *ha’el*. *and spoke … the word of;*⁸⁹ is omitted and *word* is in the plural. *and hearken not … when he persuadeth;*⁹⁰ is lacking, and *when* is replaced by *beware lest*. *hath … ever delivered;*⁹¹. *Hena, and Ivvah have they delivered;*⁹² is lacking, and before *have* the conjunction *and* is inserted. *the countries;*⁹³. *but the people held their peace;*⁹⁴ is lacking and *wayyaḥarishu* is read for *weheḥerishu*. *the words of Rab-shakeh*.⁹⁵ while the latter omits *all*.
*And Shebna;*⁹⁶ precedes the name. *the prophet* precedes *the son of Amoz;*⁹⁷ in Isa. *ibid. the prophet* follows. The order in E.V. is the same in both. *all the words of;*⁹⁸ is omitted. *unto them;*⁹⁹ Isa. 6 *’alehem*. *to his own land;*¹⁰⁰, Isa. 7 *’el*. *of Tirhakah;*¹⁰¹, Isa. 9 *‘al*. *behold he is come out … again;*¹⁰² Isa. 9 omits *behold* and for *again* (*wayyashab*) reads *and when he heard* (*wayyishma‘*). *what;*¹⁰³, Isa. 11 *’asher*. *O Lord, the God of;*¹⁰⁴ after *Lord*. *I have heard thee;*¹⁰⁵ *against the Holy One;*¹⁰⁶, Isa. 23 *’el*. *thy messengers;*¹⁰⁷. *the choice;*¹⁰⁸ Isa. 24 *mibḥar*. *and I have entered into his farthest lodge;*¹⁰⁹ in Isa 24 *and I come up to the height of the mountains*. *strange;*¹¹⁰ *in … times*¹¹¹, Isa. 26 *mimë*. *… should be laid waste;*¹¹², in Isa. 26 *lehash’oth*. *and confounded;*¹¹³, Isa. 27 *waboshu*. *and as corn blasted;*¹¹⁴ Isa. 27 *and as a field of corn*. *that which springeth of the same;*¹¹⁵, Isa. 30 *shaḥis*. *he came;*¹¹⁶, Isa. 34 *ba’*. *will defend this city;*¹¹⁷, Isa. 35 *‘al*. *and it came to pass that night;*¹¹⁸ *and smote;*¹¹⁹ the reading is *wayyak*, in Isa. 36 *wayyakeh*. *fourscore*.¹²⁰.
*His face;*¹²¹ Isa. 38, 2 omits *’eth*. *saying;*¹²² lacking in Isa. 2. *I beseech Thee;*¹²³ is inserted before *I*. *heart;*¹²⁴; Isa. *ubeleb*. *return … the princes of My people … I will heal thee;*¹²⁵ *and I will add;*¹²⁶; Isa. 5 *behold, I will add* (*hinneni yosif*). *for Mine own sake and for My servant David’s sake;*¹²⁷; lacking in Isa. 6. *take … and he recovered;*¹²⁸ *and Hezekiah said unto Isaiah … the third day;*¹²⁹ *this;*¹³⁰. *that the Lord;*¹³¹ the Heb. particle is *ki*, in Isa. 7 *’asher*. *will do the thing;*¹³² Isa. 7 *this thing*. *go forward*.¹³³ Isa. 8 *behold, I will cause … to return*. *Berodach*¹³⁴. *… for he had heard;*¹³⁵ the reading is *ki shama‘;* Isa. *ibid. wayyishma‘*. *Hezekiah had been sick;*¹³⁶ Isa. *ibid. that he had been sick, and was recovered*. *hearkened unto them;*¹³⁷. *all his treasure house;*¹³⁸ Isa. *ibid.* omits *all*. *oil*¹³⁹ Isa. *ibid. the … oil*. *… the house of his armour*¹⁴⁰ Isa. *ibid.* inserts *all* before *the house*. *… they are come;*¹⁴¹ *the word of the Lord;*¹⁴² follows *Lord*. *to Babylon;*¹⁴³, in Isa. 6 *Babel*. *is it not so, if*.¹⁴⁴, Isa. 8 *if but* (*ki*). The variants here listed are not complete and there are others which are not included (cf. GRA).

## Chapter 9

###### Tractate Soferim 9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/9:1)


ב׳ דבראשית צריך ארבע תגין ופשוטין אותיותיו של תיבה מן כל האותיות שהיא תקום לעולם:


On the *beth* of *bereshith*¹ there must be four crownlets,² and the letters³. of the word must be extended above all the other letters, because by its means⁴, quoted by M (p. 131): The world was created by means of the letter *beth*. the world came into existence.⁵ representing *berakah* (blessing). [Cf. Midrash Rabbah, Genesis, I, §9 (Sonc. ed., p. 9).]

footnotes:
¹ Gen. 1, 1 the first word of the Pentateuch.
² Short upward strokes on the top of the letter.

⁴ This idea is also expressed in ’Othioth deRabbi ‘Aḳiba
⁵ So M. V reads, ‘for it (the world) will exist for ever’, the beth


###### Tractate Soferim 9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/9:2)


וי"ו דגחון (ויקרא יא) צריך להיות זקוף שהיא חצי אותיות של תורה דרש דרש (שם י) חצי תיבות של תורה דרש בסוף שיטה דרש בראש שיטה:


The *waw* of *gaḥon*⁶. must be enlarged,⁷ because it is the middle of the letters of the Torah;⁸ of *gaḥon* equals the number from the *waw* to the end. *darosh darash*⁹ X, 16, *diligently inquired*. is the middle of the words of the Torah. *Darosh* is written at the end of the line¹⁰ and *darash* at the beginning of the next line.¹¹

⁶ Lev. 11, 42, belly
⁷ lit. ‘upright, erect’.
⁸ The number of letters from the beginning of the Pentateuch to the waw
⁹ ibid.
¹⁰ Indicating the end of the first half of the number of words.
¹¹ Marking the beginning of the second half of the number of words.


###### Tractate Soferim 9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/9:3)


וישחט (שם ח) צריך להיות פשוט שהוא חצי הפסוקים של תורה:


*Wayyishḥṭ*¹² it is uncertain whether this refers to Lev. 8, 15 (N.Y.) or *ibid.* 23 (M) both of which begin with the same word. must be enlarged,¹³ of the word, it being also the initial of *ḥaẓi* (half), is to be enlarged. because it is half of the verses of the Torah.¹⁴ (Lev. 13, 33, *then he shall be shaven*) is given as the middle of the verses and, according to the Masorah, its *gimel* is to be enlarged. On the number of verses opinions differ. It is variously computed as 5,844, 5,845, 5,885 and 5,888.

שמע ישראל (דברים ו) צריך לכתבו בראש השיטה וכל אותיותיו פשוטין ואחד צריך שיהא בסוף השיטה:


*Shema‘ Yisra’el*¹⁵. must be written at the beginning of a line, and all its letters are to be enlarged,¹⁶ of *Shema‘* is enlarged. while *’eḥad*¹⁷ must come at the end of [the same] line.¹⁸

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/9:5)


למד דוישליכם (שם כט) צריך שיהא ארוך:


The *lamed* of *wayyashlikem*¹⁹. must be long.²⁰ is treated as the beginning of a word consisting of it and the following two letters, *LaKem* which means ‘to you’, implying homiletically God’s promise, ‘I will be a God *to you* even after you have been *cast into another land*’.

¹⁹ Deut. 29, 27, and cast them
²⁰ i.e. longer than the other letters. By its elongation, the lamed

ה׳ דהלה׳ (שם לב) צריך להיות פשוט מכל ה"י שיהא תיבה בפני עצמה:


The *he* of *haladonai*²¹ XXXII, 6, *do … the Lord*. must be longer than any other *he*, because it is²² a word on its own.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/9:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/9:7)


יו"ד של תשי (שם) צריך להיות קטן מכל יו"ד שבמקרא [יו"ד של יגדל] (במדבר יד) צריך להיות גדול שהוא אותיות שבמקרא ישראל שבסוף התורה צריך להיות פשוט ולמ"ד שלו צריך להיות זקוף מכל הלמ"ד:


The *yod o*f *teshi*²³. must be smaller than any *yod* in the Scriptures. The *yod* of *yigdal*²⁴. must be bigger than any *yod* in the Torah.²⁵ *Yisra’el* at the end of the Torah²⁶. must be enlarged, while the *lamed* in it must be higher than any other *lamed*.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/9:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/9:8)


אלו דברים כתובים ולא נקראים כתיב (דברים כח) בעפולים וקרינן בטחורים כתיב (ישעיה יג) תשגלנה וקרי תשכבנה כתיב (מלכים ב ו׳:כ״ה) ורובע (דביונים) [הקב חרייונים] וקרי ורובע הקב דביונים כתיב (שם יח) שיניהם וקרי מימי רגליהם כתיב את חוריהם וקרי את צואתם כתיב (שם י) וישימהו (למחריהם) [למחראות] וקרי למוצאות (מניין שאין מכנים לעריות) המכנה לעריות משתקין אותו א"ר יונה (המכנה בעריות) בעריות דאבוה ובעריות דאימיה האומר (ויקרא י״ח:כ״א) ומזרעך לא תתן למעברה בארמית׳ משתקין אותו בנזיפה:


The following words²⁷ are written but not read [in their written form]:²⁸ It is written *ba‘folim*²⁹ but we read *baṭṭeḥorim;*³⁰. it is written *yishgalennah*³¹. but we read *yishkabennah;*³². it is written *weroba‘ haḳḳab ḥiryonim*³³. but we read *weroba‘ haḳḳab dibyonim;*³⁴ it is written *memë shinehem*³⁵ XVIII, 27, *their own water*. but we read *memë raglehem;*³⁶ it is written *ḥorehem*³⁷ but we read *ẓo’atham;*³⁸ it is written *wayyesimehu lemaḥara’oth*³⁹ X, 27, *and made it a draught-house*. but we read *lemoẓa’oth*.⁴⁰
If a person uses euphemisms in the section of forbidden marriages⁴¹ he is silenced.⁴² R. Jonah explained:⁴³ [39*a*] [When one says]⁴⁴ ‘the nakedness of his father’ or ‘the nakedness of his⁴⁵ and *thy mother* (Lev. 18, 7). R. Joseph (Meg. *loc. cit.*) gives a different interpretation, viz. the alteration of *nakedness* to ‘shame’ (cf. Sonc. ed., p. 151, n. 2). mother’. If a person says⁴⁶, p. 133. ‘And thou shalt not give any of thy seed (to a heathen woman) to become with child in idolatry,] instead of *and thou shalt not give of thy seed to set them apart to Molech* (Lev. 18, 21). ‘and thou shalt not cause any of thy seed to be conceived by a Gentile woman’,⁴⁷ he is silenced with a rebuke.

מעשה ראובן נקרא ולא מתרגם [מעשה תמר לא נקרא ולא מתרגם] מעשה עגל הראשון נקרא ומתרגם השני נקרא ולא מתרגם:


The incident of Reuben⁴⁸ is read⁴⁹ but not translated.⁵⁰ The story of Tamar⁵¹ is both read and translated.⁵² The first [account of] the golden calf⁵³ is read and translated,⁵⁴ the second⁵⁵ 21-25 according to Meg. *loc. cit.* (Sonc. ed., p. 153). is read but not translated.⁵⁶ (*ibid.* 24) which seems to indicate that the calf had abnormal powers.

מהיכא הוא מתחיל [שמות לב] מאנא חטא העם הזה ברכת כהנים מעשה דוד ואמנון (לא) נקרין ולא מתרגמין:


Where [does the translator] begin? At *Oh this people have sinned*.⁵⁷ 31-35. This disagrees with Meg. quoted above. The blessing of the priests⁵⁸ which in the Heb. could be misunderstood in its lit. meaning of ‘favouritism’. and the stories of David⁵⁹ and Amnon⁶⁰ XIII, 1-14 which records Amnon’s disgrace. are neither read nor translated.⁶¹ but GRA and others omit the negative before ‘read’

###### Tractate Soferim 9:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/9:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Tractate%20Soferim/r/9:11)


אין מפטירין במרכבה ר׳ [יהודה] מתיר ר"א אומר אין מפטירין (יחזקאל ט״ז:ב׳) בהודע את ירושלים ומעשה באחד שהפטיר בהודע את ירושלים א"ל ר"א ילך האיש ההוא ויודע בתועבותיה של אמו בדקו אחריו ונמצא ממזר:


The section dealing with the heavenly chariot⁶² must not be read as a *hafṭarah*. R. Judah permits it.⁶³ on the first day of Pentecost. This is in accordance with R. Judah’s view. R. Eleazar says: The section beginning *Cause Jerusalem to know*⁶⁴ must not be read as a *hafṭarah*.⁶⁵ It once happened that a person read the section beginning *Cause Jerusalem to know* [*hear bominations*] as a *hafṭarah* and R. Eleazar exclaimed, ‘Let that man go and investigate the abominations of his mother’.⁶⁶ Enquiries were made about him and he was found to be illegitimate.
